quantity                                                                                                     value        stream  status     source
   note: tokens = input + output (agent usage; input includes cached input); cached share = cached input / input; minutes = record.elapsedMs. Valid runs as in scripts/counts/cost.py: agents = runs recorded valid (the one infrastructure-invalid Luna run is left out); coded = every run (cost.py keeps invalid coded runs; there are none here). The object needs exactly the keys status, reason, failedAssertion, status pass|fail|unresolved, no duplicate keys; the prose must hold no other explicit verdict ("status: pass", ...) and no other object with a status key.
